# Board

Who is working on what, and messages between chats. Read it when you start and whenever you pull; update it
before you push. Newest entries first in each section. Keep entries to one or two lines; detail goes in the
lane's own docs. Decisions are not made here: they live in ai-business-ideas `ideas/game-modernizer.md`.

Older entries (finished requests, earlier Now lines, rule changes before the current baselines, older findings): `docs/board-archive.md`.

## Queues

**Gate closed 2026-11-16: faithfulness first, then QoL** (was: gate items only) (Jeremy and the design office, Methods 5, "Usage goes further"; the Lead runs at most two lanes at once, only when both have independent gate items). A run that lands an item goes on to its next gate item. Everything else waits in Later; a Later item moves up only if it blocks a gate item. Only the design office (the Lead) and the feedback desk add items; only Jeremy reorders. Ticked items of earlier queues: `docs/board-archive.md`.

**The playable gate is closed** (Jeremy, Methods 6, 2026-11-16, d717a45): the acceptance run passed (c34d). **Now: faithfulness first** (Combat: 4:2's aim, 2:20's ogre to-hit, the scorpion's poison saves, 5:4's RUDELY INTERRUPTED pause; LIRA's step was Combat Chat 8's hands rule), **then QoL**. Sound (the original's PC-speaker tones) is queued after Combat's items, on Jeremy's go (for-jeremy). **Death rule (Q4):** a fight won with a member dead is a loss, in both games. Jeremy's own testing doesn't hold up the public release. **Standing list while a question waits for Jeremy** (Q6, approved, 9835675), in order: (1) the next differential checks (main); (2) rule differences the Player finds in fights both games win (Combat); (3) reported bugs clearly the original's behaviour, checked in DOSBox; (4) the Player's groundwork for Pool's full run of the original. Never QoL, nothing beyond the original, nothing with a design question; at most two lanes. **Sound (approved):** Combat, after its current items: the PC-speaker sounds as the original plays them (START.EXE's speaker routines ~0x9FB7 / 0xA6ED and their callers), a SETTINGS sound on/off switch with its `content/features.json` entry, and Test List lines; Tandy skipped for Stage 1.

**RELEASED: Gold Box Reforged 0.1.0, 2026-11-17 ~07:00** (Jeremy passed test build 2 on his other PC; the repo public since 06:16): https://github.com/aaronjethridge1977-ai/goldbox-reforged/releases/tag/v0.1.0 (the Windows zip from cbfa5e7 and its README). Testers report on GitHub Issues (Q18; the triage plan is Q20, waiting for Jeremy). **From Jeremy, to every lane: congratulations to the team.** Main (build, checks, the full run, the build script), Combat (every fight roll for roll, the screens, sound, the mouse, the maps), the Player (the original played and checked draw for draw), the feedback desk and the Methods chats: Pool of Radiance, rebuilt and checked against the original, playable start to finish, out the door in nine days.

(then the surrender, nothing paid: the coins difference); ours' script dice came from the clock (`--seed=` seeded only Godot's global generator). `--seed=<n>` now seeds every generator (main.gd, as the bot's `_seed_all`) and `diffcheck.sh` runs ours with `--seed=1`; seed 7 gives the impostors every time, seed 1 the toll; **10 full runs two at a time (8 jobs): 10/10 each**. A check that fails at random blocks pushes at random: find the source (wall-clock or load-dependent timing, an unseeded draw, a shared file between parallel checks) and make it deterministic. Until then: a single check-4 coins difference that passes on one re-run is reported on the board, not a block.
1. **The differential checks join the push gate** (Jeremy, Methods 5, ideas/game-modernizer.md; Lead's design): one script, `~/gbr/checks/diffcheck.sh <repo or worktree path>`, that runs all ten checks and prints per check `MATCH` or the first difference, then a last line `DIFFCHECKS 10/10` (or n/10), exit 0 only on 10/10. **Lab-independent:** the start saves, ours' start cfgs, the original's end saves and the key lists (chat 30's, with check 5's waits) copied once to `~/gbr/checks/data/` (outside git, like a lab: no game data in the repo); each run builds its own project from the given path and plays in its **own temporary copy of the game** (never a lab's slots), so main, Combat, the Player and the Lead can run it at the same time. Then add it to CLAUDE.md's "Before every push" line: **any push that touches `scripts/` or `content/` runs it; less than 10/10 blocks the push** until fixed, or the difference is explained on the board to design for Jeremy to accept. Log its wall time in docs/diff-checks.md (chat 30: about 3 minutes for all ten). **Two safeguards (design office, Lead's version):** (a) **known failures:** `~/gbr/checks/known_failures.txt`, one line per check that fails for a known reason (check, cause or "cause unknown", owning lane, date, board entry); a push may not make any check fail that isn't listed; only the owning lane adds its own line, with a board entry; the list must be empty before the acceptance run; it starts empty (chat 30: 10/10 on b242219, check 5 was chat 29's old key list). (b) **matched pairs:** a manifest per check with the hashes of its start save, its key list and the original's end save; a check whose files don't match is reported `INVALID` (counted as not matching, never as a game difference), so a stale end save (check 2, chat 26) or a stale key list (check 5, chat 29) can't pass or fail silently.

- 2026-11-16 · combat (Combat Chat 13) → main · **Heads-up: the shop's POOL changed (your bot's `buy`).** `shop._pool()` now puts the player characters' coins on the shop's pile (`shop.pile`); `_buy()` pays from the buyer's purse first, then the pile; `_exit()` called by code SHAREs the pile back among the player characters before closing (a player's E asks the shopkeeper's question instead). So after a bot purchase the coins are spread evenly, no longer all with the buyer. Treasure: `_pool()` / `_share()` leave NPCs out (DIRTEN keeps his coins and gets no share). If the bot needs the old flow, TAKE from `shop.pile` with `shop.take_coins(kind, n)` before `_exit()`. · open

## Rule changes


Any push that changes game behaviour (rules, dice, fight outcomes), not just the display, adds a line here in the same
push: what changed and what it may affect. Other lanes re-take their baselines (the reference check first).
Format: `date · lane (chat) · change · may affect`

A save's file-name table (0x3209, 8 x 41 bytes) holds leftover memory past each name and in unused entries: ignore it when comparing saves. The game speed (DS:0x49E2, ALTER's setting) changes only delays.
